[-07-21 09:57] TEXT: Based on the project structure, here's how I would approach updating upstream dependencies (Cherry Studio and MiMo Code): ## Update Strategy ### 1. **Cherry Studio (`@cherrystudio/*`)** These are **workspace packages** (`workspace:*`), meaning they're local packages within the monorepo: ```json "@cherrystudio/ai-core": "workspace:*", "@cherrystudio/provider-registry": "workspace:*", "@cherrystudio/ui": "workspace:*", "@cherrystudio/extension-table-plus": "workspace:^" ``` **Update approach:** - Sync with upstream `CherryHQ/cherry-studio` repo - Run `pnpm update` after merging upstream changes - The `workspace:*` protocol means versions are resolved locally, so updates are controlled by the workspace itself ### 2. **MiMo Code (`@mimo-ai/sdk`)** Currently pinned to version `0.1.5`: ```json "@mimo-ai/sdk": "0.1.5" ``` **Update approach:** - Check for new versions on npm: `npm view @mimo-ai/sdk versions` - Update in package.json with exact version pinning - Run `pnpm install` and test thoroughly ### 3. **General Update Workflow** ```bash # 1. Check for updates pnpm outdated # 2. Update specific packages pnpm update @mimo-ai/sdk@latest pnpm update @cherrystudio/analytics-client@latest # 3. Run tests pnpm test pnpm lint pnpm typecheck # 4. Build and verify pnpm build ``` ### 4. **Risk Mitigation** - **Pin exact versions** for critical dependencies (like `@mimo-ai/sdk`) - **Test thoroughly** after updates - run full test suite - **Check changelogs** for breaking changes - **Update one package at a time** to isolate issues - **Use lockfiles** (`pnpm-lock.yaml`) to ensure reproducible builds ### 5. **Workspace Packages** For `@cherrystudio/*` workspace packages, updates are controlled by: - Syncing with upstream `cherry-studio` repo - Running `pnpm install` to resolve workspace dependencies - Testing integration with the rest of the codebase The key is to update incrementally, test at each step, and maintain compatibility with the rest of the project.

[-07-21 09:58] TEXT: ## 上游依赖更新策略 ### 1. **Cherry Studio (`@cherrystudio/*`)** 这些是 **workspace 本地包**（`workspace:*`），版本在 monorepo 内部管理： ```json "@cherrystudio/ai-core": "workspace:*", "@cherrystudio/provider-registry": "workspace:*", "@cherrystudio/ui": "workspace:*", "@cherrystudio/extension-table-plus": "workspace:^" ``` **更新方式：** - 从上游 `CherryHQ/cherry-studio` 仓库同步代码 - 合并上游变更后运行 `pnpm install` - 由于使用 `workspace:*` 协议，版本由本地 workspace 控制 ### 2. **MiMo Code (`@mimo-ai/sdk`)** 当前固定版本为 `0.1.5`： ```json "@mimo-ai/sdk": "0.1.5" ``` **更新方式：** - 检查 npm 上的新版本：`npm view @mimo-ai/sdk versions` - 在 `package.json` 中更新到具体版本 - 运行 `pnpm install` 并充分测试 ### 3. **通用更新流程** ```bash # 1. 检查可用更新 pnpm outdated # 2. 更新特定包 pnpm update @mimo-ai/sdk@latest pnpm update @cherrystudio/analytics-client@latest # 3. 运行测试 pnpm test pnpm lint pnpm typecheck # 4. 构建验证 pnpm build ``` ### 4. **风险控制** - **精确锁定版本**：关键依赖（如 `@mimo-ai/sdk`）使用精确版本号 - **充分测试**：更新后运行完整测试套件 - **查看变更日志**：检查是否有破坏性变更 - **逐个更新**：一次只更新一个包，便于定位问题 - **使用锁文件**：通过 `pnpm-lock.yaml` 确保构建可复现 ### 5. **Workspace 包更新** 对于 `@cherrystudio/*` 本地包，更新由以下方式控制： - 从上游 `cherry-studio` 仓库同步代码 - 运行 `pnpm install` 解析 workspace 依赖 - 测试与项目其他部分的集成 **核心原则：** 增量更新、逐步测试、保持兼容性。

[-07-21 09:59] TEXT: ## 破坏性更新的处理策略 ### 1. **评估阶段** ```bash # 1. 查看变更日志 npm view @mimo-ai/sdk --json | jq '.repository' # 访问 GitHub releases 页面查看 breaking changes # 2. 检查类型定义变化 pnpm install @mimo-ai/sdk@latest --dry-run pnpm typecheck 2>&1 | head -50 # 3. 运行测试查看失败情况 pnpm test 2>&1 | grep -A 5 "FAIL\|Error" ``` ### 2. **渐进式迁移** #### **阶段 A：准备** ```bash # 创建专门的迁移分支 git checkout -b chore/update-mimo-sdk-0.2 # 备份当前工作 git stash ``` #### **阶段 B：更新依赖** ```bash # 更新到新版本 pnpm update @mimo-ai/sdk@latest # 检查类型错误 pnpm typecheck:node pnpm typecheck:web ``` #### **阶段 C：逐个修复** **修复类型错误：** ```typescript // 旧代码 const client = new MimoClient({ apiKey: 'xxx' }) // 新代码（假设 API 变更） const client = new MimoClient({ apiKey: 'xxx', // 新增必需参数 region: 'us-east-1' }) ``` **修复运行时错误：** ```typescript // 旧代码 const result = await client.chat({ model: 'gpt-4' }) // 新代码（假设方法签名变更） const result = await client.chat({ model: 'gpt-4', // 新增必需参数 temperature: 0.7 }) ``` ### 3. **处理策略矩阵** | 变更类型 | 处理方式 | 示例 | |---------|---------|------| | **API 签名变更** | 更新所有调用点 | `method(a, b)` → `method({ a, b })` | | **类型变更** | 更新类型定义 | `string` → `string \| null` | | **返回值变更** | 更新解构/使用 | `result.data` → `result.data.items` | | **配置变更** | 更新初始化代码 | 新增必需配置项 | | **方法重命名** | 全局搜索替换 | `getData()` → `fetchData()` | ### 4. **自动化修复工具** ```bash # 使用 TypeScript 的 --fix（如果支持） pnpm typecheck --fix # 使用 ESLint 自动修复 pnpm lint --fix # 使用 codemods（如果有） npx @mimo-ai/sdk-codemods migrate ``` ### 5. **手动修复流程** ```typescript // 1. 找到所有错误 pnpm typecheck 2>&1 | grep "error TS" // 2. 逐个文件修复 # 修复 src/main/ai/client.ts # 修复 src/main/ai/handler.ts # 修复 src/renderer/hooks/useAi.ts // 3. 验证修复 pnpm typecheck pnpm test ``` ### 6. **回滚策略** ```bash # 如果修复太复杂，可以回滚 git checkout package.json pnpm-lock.yaml pnpm install # 或者使用 git stash git stash pop ``` ### 7. **测试验证清单** ```bash # 1. 类型检查 pnpm typecheck # 2. 单元测试 pnpm test # 3. Lint 检查 pnpm lint # 4. 构建验证 pnpm build # 5. 运行时测试 pnpm dev # 手动测试关键功能 ``` ### 8. **文档更新** ```markdown # 更新 CHANGELOG.md ## [Unreleased] ### Changed - Updated `@mimo-ai/sdk` from 0.1.5 to 0.2.0 - Updated all AI client code to use new API ### Migration Notes - `MimoClient` constructor now requires `region` parameter - `chat()` method signature changed ``` ### 9. **团队协作** ```bash # 1. 创建详细的 PR 描述 git commit -m "chore(deps): update @mimo-ai/sdk to 0.2.0 - Updated client initialization code - Fixed 15 type errors - Updated 8 test files - All tests passing Breaking changes handled: - Added required 'region' parameter - Updated chat() method signature" # 2. 请求代码审查 # 3. 在 CI 中验证 ``` ### 10. **预防措施** ```json // package.json 中使用精确版本 "@mimo-ai/sdk": "0.1.5" // 不用 ^0.1.5 // 使用 lockfile 确保可复现 "packageManager": "pnpm@11.8.0+sha512...." ``` **核心原则：** 小步快跑、充分测试、保留回滚能力、文档先行。

[]).filter is not a function` occurs when the left side of `??` is **truthy but not an array** (so `??` does not trigger, and `.filter` doesn't exist on the value). ## Primary Suspects (settings page path) All three locations use the same pattern with `useProviderApiKeys`: ### 1. `src/renderer/pages/settings/ProviderSettings/ModelList/useAutoPullOnApiKeyChange.ts` — Line 23 ```typescript (apiKeysData?.keys ?? []) .filter((key) => key.isEnabled) ``` ### 2. `src/renderer/pages/settings/ProviderSettings/hooks/useProviderPullReconcile.ts` — Line 26 ```typescript (apiKeysData?.keys ?? []) .filter((key) => key.isEnabled) ``` ### 3. `src/renderer/pages/settings/ProviderSettings/hooks/providerSetting/getModelSyncSignature.ts` — Line 8 ```typescript (apiKeysData?.keys ?? []) .filter((key) => key.isEnabled) ``` All three consume `data` from `useProviderApiKeys` (`C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\renderer\hooks\useProvider.ts`, line 249-251), which calls `useQuery('/providers/:providerId/api-keys')`. The schema (`C:\Users\chenfei\Documents\GitHub\prism-agent\src\shared\data\api\schemas\providers.ts`, line 198) says the response is `{ keys: ApiKeyEntry[] }`, so `apiKeysData?.keys` should be an array. **Why it breaks:** If the SWR cache or backend ever returns a value where `apiKeysData` is truthy but `apiKeysData.keys` is a truthy non-array (e.g., a string, number, or object without an array), the `?? []` fallback does **not** trigger, and `.filter()` fails. This happens specifically when: - `apiKeysData` is `{ keys: "some_string" }` or similar unexpected shape - The IPC/DataApi layer returns a cached error object or malformed response ### Secondary suspect (also fires on settings open) ### 4. `src/renderer/components/ModelSelector/useModelSelectorData.ts` — Line 276 ```typescript const filteredModels = (tagFilteredModelsByProvider.get(provider.id) ?? []).filter(...) ``` `tagFilteredModelsByProvider` is a `Map<string, Model[]>` (line 238-243), populated with arrays, so this should be safe. However, the `ModelSelector` is used in `ModelSettings` which could be opened from settings. ## Recommended Fix Add `Array.isArray()` guards instead of relying on `?? []` alone: ```typescript // Before (unsafe if keys is truthy non-array): (apiKeysData?.keys ?? []).filter((key) => key.isEnabled) // After (safe): (apiKeysData?.keys?.filter((key) => key.isEnabled) ?? []) ``` Or more defensively: ```typescript (Array.isArray(apiKeysData?.keys) ? apiKeysData.keys : []).filter(...) ``` The optional chaining approach (`?.filter`) is the cleanest fix — if `apiKeysData?.keys` is not an array, the chain short-circuits to `undefined`, and `?? []` then correctly provides the empty array fallback.
